\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
The \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} class This class holds the tools to manage the whole \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} structure. 

Instantiating this class allow to benefit from any underneeth objects. There is one \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} object per \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file}{ZRandom\+File} physical structure. \begin{DoxySeeAlso}{See also}
\doxylink{group___z_r_f_physical}{ZRandom\+File Physical layer} 
\end{DoxySeeAlso}

\+\_\+pool\+Delete Moves block at rank p\+ZBATRank from ZBAT to ZFBT. State is set to ZBS\+\_\+\+Deleted. ZFBT is ordered upon block Addres. If p\+Keep\+ZBAT is set, then ZBAT element remains available. If not, ZBAT element is removed. Returns ZFBT rank of deleted block.
